## Pairwise Statistical Comparisons

| Config A | Config B | Δ mean | p-value | Significant (α=0.05) |
|----------|----------|--------|---------|----------------------|
| none | nsd | +0.3094 | 0.013 | ✅ YES |
| none | sd | +0.2621 | 0.006 | ✅ YES |
| additive_uniform | nsd | -0.0222 | 0.000 | ✅ YES |
| nsd | sd | -0.0473 | 0.000 | ✅ YES |
| additive_uniform | sd | -0.0695 | 0.000 | ✅ YES |
| additive_uniform | none | -0.3316 | 0.004 | ✅ YES |
